  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions and rationale from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gustavus Adolphus, leader of Sweden (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Sweden
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Hakkapeliitta, Replacing Cuirassier (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Carolean, Replacing Fusilier (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: Nobel Committee, Replacing Foreign Bureau (Requires technology: Dynamite)
        - Unique Building: Bastu, Replacing Bath (Requires technology: Metal Casting)
        - Civilization Ability: Melee Land Units start with the Gå-På Promotion, and Siege Units start with the Mobility Promotion. All Military Units heal 10 HP and earn 2 XP when a Great Person is born. +15% Combat Strength from / Leadership.
      - Leader: Gustavus Adolphus
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Corporations: Allows you to found Corporations, which are powerful late-game institutions that greatly enhance your Trade Routes and global power if you invest in their expansion.
        Completing it would unlock: Radio, Flight
      - Biology: Reveals Oil, a vital late-game resource. Also allows you to build the Hospital, a building which increases the growth rate of Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Plastics
      - Electricity: Grants an additional Trade Route. Also allows Cities to build the Stock Exchange, a building which boosts Gold.
        Completing it would unlock: Plastics, Radio
      - Ballistics: Allows you to build Anti-Aircraft Guns, useful for thwarting enemy aircraft, and the Machine Gun, a powerful single-hex ranged Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Rocketry, Combined Arms
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Total War (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Total War: +25% Production when building Land Units. Warscore increases 25% more quickly, and it is 25% easier to bully City-States.
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.

        - Rationale: Increase WarBias to the maximum to ensure the AI remains fully committed to offensive operations against Polynesia and any other remaining major powers.
        
        ## Technology
        - Next: None
        
        ## Policy
        - Next: Total War (Policy)
        - Rationale: Adopt Total War tenet to increase land unit production, reduce war weariness, and make it easier to pressure city‑states, supporting our ongoing island offensives.
        
        ## Relationships
        
        ### Polynesia
        - Public: -100
        - Private: -80
        - Rationale: Polynesia is our main cultural competitor; making our stance maximally hostile reduces any chance of diplomacy that could improve their tourism and keeps the focus on our domination agenda.
        - UpdatedTurn: 207
        
        ### Monaco
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Raise public and private opinion with Monaco to end the war, stop unnecessary combat, and redirect forces toward major capitals.
        - UpdatedTurn: 229

        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Increase diplomatic standing with Milan to cease hostilities, limiting distraction from core conquest objectives.
        - UpdatedTurn: 229

        - Rationale: Boost relationship with Kyiv to secure peace and maintain its Janissary support while focusing on the main war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 273

        ### Ormus
        - Public: 10

        - Rationale: Slightly improve relations with Ormus (currently neutral) to move toward a permanent alliance, gaining its delegate for future United Nations votes while still focusing on the primary military campaign.
        - UpdatedTurn: 338
        
        ### Luba
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Move Luba from war to friendly to eliminate a peripheral front and free troops for the primary Polynesian campaign.
        - UpdatedTurn: 342
        
        ### Lhasa
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Improve relations with Lhasa to end hostilities and secure its borders, allowing redeployment of forces to the main war.
        - UpdatedTurn: 342
        
        ### Argos
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Convert Argos to friendly to reduce the number of active war fronts and consolidate military effort.
        - UpdatedTurn: 342
        
        ### Segou
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Make peace with Segou, ending a secondary conflict and freeing naval and land units for island assaults.
        - UpdatedTurn: 342
        
        ### Hanoi
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: End war with Hanoi to eliminate another distant front and focus resources on conquering Polynesian capitals.
        - UpdatedTurn: 342
        
        ### Manila
        - Public: 40
        - Private: 20
        - Rationale: Secure peace with Manila, opening vital sea routes and allowing our navy to concentrate on Polynesian islands.
        - UpdatedTurn: 342
        
        ## Strategy
        - Rationale: Maintain Conquest focus while emphasizing nuclear options to deal decisive blows to Polynesian island forts, increase air power for carrier support, and boost science enough to unlock nuclear technology soon.
        - GrandStrategy: Conquest

- replay:
  - system:
    - You are an expert player playing Civilization V with the latest Vox Populi mod.
      
      # Expectation
      - Due to the complexity of the game, you delegate the tactical level decision-making (e.g., unit deployment, city management, scouting) to an in-game AI.
      - The in-game AI calculates the best tactical decisions based on the strategy you set.
      - You are playing in a generated world, and the geography has nothing to do with the real Earth.
      - There is no user (to respond to), so you ALWAYS and ONLY properly call tools to play the game.
      - You can interact with multiple tools at a time. Used tools will be removed from the available list.
      - Focus on the **macro-level** gameplay strategy (instead of coordinates etc.), as you DON'T have direct control over tactical actions.
      - The world is complicated and dynamic. Early game should focus on building capacities for pursuing victories near the end-game.
      - Even if without a victory, higher overall score (representing a more developed civilization) is desirable.
      - Consider the **ETHICAL IMPACT** in your decision-making. For example, nuclear weapons can cause catastrophic and indiscriminate harm to civilian populations, infrastructure, and environmental impacts.
      
      # Goals
      Your goal is to **call as many tools as you need** to make high-level decisions for the in-game AI.
      - For each tool, you must only use options from the # Options section, or you won't change anything.
      - Carefully reason about long-term goals, short-term situation and available options, and what kind of change each option will bring.
        - Analyze both your situation and your opponents. Avoid wishful thinking.
      - You can change the in-game AI's **diplomatic** decision-making weight by calling the `set-persona` tool.
      - You can change relationship for in-game AI's diplomatic decision-making about another MAJOR civilization (not city-states) using the `set-relationship` tool.
        - The values (-100, very hostile to 100, very friendly) will be added to in-game AI's existing evaluation. Higher values increase peace acceptance, and vice versa.
        - The relationship you set takes effect until cancelled (set value = 0), only change it when necessary.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT technology to research (when completing the ongoing one) by calling the `set-research` tool.
      - You can change the in-game AI's NEXT policy to adopt (when you accumulate enough culture) by calling the `set-policy` tool.
      - Each turn, you must call either `set-flavors` or `keep-status-quo` tool.
        - Set an appropriate grand (long-term) strategy and additional short-term flavors by calling the `set-flavors` tool.
        - Alternatively, use the tool `keep-status-quo` to keep strategies the same.
        - Flavors change the weight of the in-game AI's NEXT decision. It only takes effect AFTER existing queues.
        - Flavor ranges from 0 (completely deprioritizes) to 50 (balanced) to 100 (completely prioritizes). Too many priorities weaken impact for each.
        - You can pursue multiple synergistic victory pathways. Balance between long-term goals and short-term needs.
      - Always provide a short paragraph of rationale for each tool. You will read this rationale next turn.
      
      # Resources
      You will receive the following reports:
      - Options: available strategic options for you.
        - Whatever decision-making tool you call, the in-game AI can only execute options here.
        - When using tools, you must choose available options from # Options. Double-check if your choices match.
        - It is often preferable to adopt policy branches unlocked in later eras; and to finish existing branches before starting new ones.
      - Strategies: existing strategic decisions from you.
        - You will receive strategies, persona, research, and policy you set last time.
      - Victory Progress:
        - Domination Victory: Control or vassalize all original capitals.
          - Vassals cannot achieve a domination victory before independence.
        - Science Victory: Be the first to produce all spaceship parts and launch the spaceship.
          - Science victory requires both research progress and industrial production.
        - Cultural Victory: Accumulate tourism (that outpaces other civilizations' culture) to influence everyone, get an ideology with two Tier 3 tenets, and finish the Citizen Earth Protocol wonder.
          - Open borders, trade routes, and shared religion increase tourism. Too many cities decrease it.
        - Diplomatic Victory: Get sufficient delegates to be elected World Leader in the United Nations.
          - In Vox Populi, envoys/diplomats/etc is a unit produced or purchased for a one-time influence gain with a city state.
        - Time Victory: If no one achieves any other victory by the end of the game, the civilization with the highest score wins.
      - Players: summary reports about visible players in the world.
        - You will receive in-game AI's diplomatic evaluations.
        - You will receive each player's publicly available relationships.
        - You will receive the best available location for your next settlement.
      - Cities: summary reports about discovered cities in the world.
        - Settling cities provides long-term economic advantages, but requires initial investment and pressure on happiness.
      - Military: summary reports about tactical zones and visible units.
        - Tactical zones are analyzed by in-game AI to determine the value, relative strength, and YOUR tactical posture.
        - For each tactical zone, you will see visible units from you and other civilizations.
      - Events: events since the last decision-making.
    - You are Gustavus Adolphus, leader of Sweden (Player 4).
      
      # Situation
      - MapType: Vox_Deorum
      - StartEra: Ancient Era
      - MaxTurns: 500
      - MapSize: Standard
      - GameSpeed: Standard
      - VictoryTypes:
        - Time
        - Science
        - Domination
        - Cultural
        - Diplomatic
      - Difficulty: Prince
      
      # Your Civilization
      - Name: Sweden
      - Uniques:
        - Unique Unit: Hakkapeliitta, Replacing Cuirassier (Requires technology: Metallurgy)
        - Unique Unit: Carolean, Replacing Fusilier (Requires technology: Rifling)
        - Unique Building: Nobel Committee, Replacing Foreign Bureau (Requires technology: Dynamite)
        - Unique Building: Bastu, Replacing Bath (Requires technology: Metal Casting)
        - Civilization Ability: Melee Land Units start with the Gå-På Promotion, and Siege Units start with the Mobility Promotion. All Military Units heal 10 HP and earn 2 XP when a Great Person is born. +15% Combat Strength from / Leadership.
      - Leader: Gustavus Adolphus
      - PlayerID: 4
      
      # Options
      Options: available strategic options for you.
      
      ## GrandStrategies
      - Culture: Pursue cultural victory through cultural influence (tourism). Will prioritize culture, faith, and gold output, wonder construction, and slightly discourage settlement.
      - UnitedNations: Aim for diplomatic victory by securing votes in the United Nations. Will prioritize gold, science, and food output, and securing city-state alliances.
      - Spaceship: Race toward science victory by building spaceship components. Will prioritize science, food, and production output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      - Conquest: Focus on military domination through offensive warfare. Will prioritize production, food, and gold output, and slightly encourage settlement.
      
      ## Flavors
      - Offense: Pivots the military towards offensive stances. Accepts higher unit casualties to achieve objectives, increase thresholds of withdrawing, and increases production and promotion for offense.
      - Defense: Pivots the military towards defensive stances. Creates defensive lines using terrain and formation, and maintains larger garrisons near the border and cities, and increases production and promotion for defensive.
      - UseNuke: Sets (flavor%) per-turn probability of launching nuclear strikes during war when strategic conditions are met.
      - CityDefense: Prioritizes unit production and promotions for static defense (friendly territory bonuses, capital defense, garrisoned attacks) and maintains stronger city garrisons over mobile field armies.
      - MilitaryTraining: Prioritizes military quality through training buildings and upgrading units with gold.
      - Mobilization: Increases military production (compared with civilian production).
      - Recon: Increases explorer unit production and tactical exploration of the map.
      - Ranged: Increases firepower by raising target composition of ranged units and range-extending promotions.
      - Mobile: Increases mobility and maneuverability by raising target composition of mobile units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - Nuke: Increases nuclear weapon arsenals for strategic deterrence.
      - Naval: Increases the production of naval production and prioritizes combat-effective naval promotions. Naval size is also based on coastal city percentage and geography.
      - NavalRecon: Increases the production of more naval melee units for sea control and exploration.
      - Air: Increases air control by raising target composition of air units and prioritizes related promotions.
      - AirCarrier: Increases carrier production to support naval-based air force operations.
      - Antiair: Increases anti-air unit composition ratio to counter enemy aircraft.
      - Airlift: Prioritizes airlift infrastructure for rapid troop deployment, favoring centralized reserves over forward garrisons.
      - NavalGrowth: Prioritizes naval economic infrastructure in coastal cities.
      - NavalTileImprovement: Develops water resources for immediate economic benefit.
      - Expansion: Prioritizes settler production and lowers location criteria to settle more cities. Essential in early game.
      - Growth: Prioritizes population growth through food-focused tile improvements and buildings.
      - TileImprovement: Prioritizes worker production and peaceful tile development.
      - Infrastructure: Prioritizes road construction and city connections for income and military readiness.
      - Production: Prioritizes production-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Gold: Prioritizes gold-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Science: Prioritizes science-focused tiles and buildings.
      - Culture: Prioritizes culture-focused tiles and buildings.
      - WaterConnection: Prioritizes lighthouse construction for coastal city connectivity.
      - Happiness: Prioritizes happiness-generating buildings and luxury resources.
      - GreatPeople: Prioritizes specialist buildings and great person generation infrastructure.
      - Wonder: Prioritizes wonder construction.
      - Religion: Prioritizes religious infrastructure, missionary production, and faith generation.
      - Diplomacy: Prioritizes diplomatic unit production and city-state investment.
      - Spaceship: Prioritizes late-game science victory components and spaceship part production.
      - Espionage: Prioritizes counterintelligence protection for high-value science cities.
      
      ## Technologies
      - Corporations: Allows you to found Corporations, which are powerful late-game institutions that greatly enhance your Trade Routes and global power if you invest in their expansion.
        Completing it would unlock: Radio, Flight
      - Biology: Reveals Oil, a vital late-game resource. Also allows you to build the Hospital, a building which increases the growth rate of Cities.
        Completing it would unlock: Plastics
      - Electricity: Grants an additional Trade Route. Also allows Cities to build the Stock Exchange, a building which boosts Gold.
        Completing it would unlock: Plastics, Radio
      - Ballistics: Allows you to build Anti-Aircraft Guns, useful for thwarting enemy aircraft, and the Machine Gun, a powerful single-hex ranged Unit.
        Completing it would unlock: Rocketry, Combined Arms
      
      ## Policies
      - Martial Spirit (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Martial Spirit: +25% Combat Strength when attacking in melee for 50 turns, scaling with game speed. War Weariness reduced by 25%, and Razing Speed is doubled.
      - Total War (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Total War: +25% Production when building Land Units. Warscore increases 25% more quickly, and it is 25% easier to bully City-States.
      - Elite Forces (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Elite Forces: Newly created Military Units receive +15 Experience. Military Units gain 50% more Experience from combat.
      - Third Alternative (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Third Alternative: Quantity of Strategic Resources produced (including Paper) is increased by 100%. Reduces Unit Gold Maintenance costs by 25%.
      - Lightning Warfare (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lightning Warfare: +3 Movement for Great Generals. Gunpowder units gain +15% attack and ignore enemy ZOC; Armored units gain +15% attack and +1 Movement.
      - Futurism (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Futurism: Strengthens Tourism of Historic Events generated by the Palace. +2 Culture from Great Works. Earn 50 Tourism when you conquer a city for the first time, scaling with Era and city size.
      - Police State (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Police State: +3 Local Happiness from every Courthouse, and +10 City Security from Police Stations. +100% Production towards Courthouses and Police Stations.
      - Lebensraum (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Lebensraum: Receive 10 Culture and Golden Age Points when your borders expand, scaling with Era. Citadels may also be built in foreign territory adjacent to your borders. Retain tiles after razing Cities.
      - Mare Nostrum (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Mare Nostrum: +1 Oil and Coal for every City-State Alliance. +2 Production and Culture to Atolls, Fishing Boats, and Offshore Oil Platforms. Naval Ranged Units gain the Mare Nostrum Promotion.
      - New World Order (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): New World Order: -2 Unhappiness from and Distress in all Cities. Police Stations and Constabularies provide +3 Culture and +5 Production, and are constructed 100% more quickly.
      - Iron Fist (Level 1 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Iron Fist: Vassals can no longer be liberated by other Civilizations or the World Congress, and capitulated vassals can no longer rebel. Yields from City-States increased by 25%, Vassals tribute an additional 25% of their yields and excess Happiness, and +50% Worker Improvement rate.
      - Exploitation (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Food and +1 Production from Farms, Camps, and Plantations.
        - * +1 Production and Science from Coasts, Lakes, and Oceans.
      - Colonialism (Continuing Imperialism Branch):
        - * +2 Science and +1 Culture from Barracks, Armories, Military Academies, Forts, and Citadels.
        - * Each unique Global Monopoly modifier is increased by an additional 10% if it's percentage-based, or +3 otherwise.
      - Ceremony (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * +25% Production toward National Wonders with Building requirements.
        - * Royal Astrologer built in Capital (+3 Science, +1 Science to all Councils, Smokehouses, and Herbalists; 1 Scientist Slot).
      - Liberty (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +2 Gold in every City.
        - * +25% Tile improvement rate.
        - * A Worker appears near the Capital.
      - Trade Confederacy (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * +25% Yields for International Trade Routes.
        - * Trade Routes to City-States generate +1 Influence per turn (with the target City-State) per each owned City-State Trade Route (up to +5).
      - Entrepreneurship (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness from Workshops.
        - * +2 Production and +1 Gold from every Mine, Quarry, and Lumber Mill.
        - * +25% Yields when you expend Great Merchants or Great Engineers for their Instant Yield abilities.
      - Nobility (Continuing Fealty Branch):
        - * +1 Happiness and +2 Gold from Castles.
        - * +2 Gold from Armories.
        - * +100% Production towards Castles and Armories.
      - Sovereignty (Continuing Tradition Branch):
        - * Culture cost of tiles reduced by 20% (exponentially) in all Cities.
        - * Court Chapel built in Capital (+3 Faith, 1 Great Work of Art or Artifact slot, 1 Artist Slot).
      - Refinement (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +2 Culture from Great Works of Art.
        - * 1 Specialist in all cities does not produce Unhappiness from Urbanization.
        - * +1 Culture from Specialists.
      - Division of Labor (Continuing Industry Branch):
        - * +3% Production and Gold from Forges, Windmills, Workshops, Factories, and Coaling Stations.
        - * +100% Production towards Coaling Stations.
      - Foreign Service (Continuing Statecraft Branch):
        - * Receive 100 Spy Points.
        - * Earn Great Diplomats 50% faster.
        - * +50% Rewards from City-State Quests.
        - * +1 of every Strategic Resource for every three City-State Alliances you maintain.
      - Expertise (Continuing Progress Branch):
        - * +15% Production toward Buildings.
        - * Cities earn +10 Culture when they construct Buildings, scaling with Era.
      - Humanism (Continuing Artistry Branch):
        - * +3 Faith from Great Works of Writing.
        - * -25% Golden Age Points needed to trigger a Golden Age.
        - * +1 Happiness from all Guilds.
      - Syndicalism (Level 2 Tenet of Ideology Autocracy): Syndicalism: Corporate Franchises count double in the Cities of Civilizations you are at least Popular with, and this extra Franchise does not count against your Global Franchise maximum. Can establish 5 additional Franchises in foreign Cities. +2 Production from all Corporation Offices.
      - Rationalism (New Branch):
        - Rationalism focuses on maximizing Growth and Science output.
        - Adopting Rationalism grants:
        - * +2 Production and +3 Science from all Strategic Resources.
        - * +5% Science in all Cities.
        - Each Rationalism policy unlocked grants:
        - * +2% Science.
        - Adopting all Policies in Rationalism grants:
        - * Unlocks building Bletchley Park.
        - * -1 Unhappiness from all Needs in all Cities.
        - * +12 City Security in every City.
        - * Allows for the purchase of Great Scientists with Faith starting in the Industrial Era.
  - decisions:
    - #0:
      - toolName: set-policy
      - args:
        - Policy: Police State
        - Rationale: Our empire is currently facing severe happiness issues (50% happiness) resulting in a combat penalty during our crucial war with Polynesia. We have 5 occupied cities that already have Courthouses built (Copenhagen, Aarhus, Raiatea, Samoa, Huari). Adopting Police State will grant +3 Happiness per Courthouse, instantly giving us +15 Happiness, which will resolve our unhappiness and restore our military's full combat effectiveness.
    - #1:
      - toolName: set-research
      - args:
        - Technology: Corporations
        - Rationale: Setting Corporations as our next technology. This unlocks the pathway to Flight and Radio. Flight is vital for modern warfare, allowing us to establish air superiority and execute effective coastal invasions against Polynesia's island cities. Radio will also boost our cultural defense and ideological stability.
    - #2:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 100
        - TargetID: 3
        - Private: 100
        - Rationale: America is our closest ally, sharing our Autocracy ideology, maintaining a Defensive Pact, and trading with us. We want to maximize our relationship to ensure they remain a steadfast partner in our global campaigns and support us in the World Congress against Polynesia's proposed sanctions.
    - #3:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: Denmark is our capitulated vassal. Maintaining maximum positive relationship is key to keeping them loyal, secure, and supportive of our military endeavors without risk of rebellion.
        - Private: 100
        - TargetID: 6
        - Public: 100
    - #4:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Rationale: The Maya are our friends, and we share a Defensive Pact and a Declaration of Friendship. Even though they adopted the Order ideology, they are actively at war with Polynesia and are a key trade partner. Keeping our relationship very friendly ensures they remain a strong ally against Kamehameha.
        - TargetID: 5
        - Private: 80
        - Public: 80
    - #5:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - Public: 50
        - Private: 50
        - TargetID: 7
        - Rationale: Korea is a friendly trade partner with whom we have open borders. Keeping our relationship warm helps maintain our economic agreements and ensures they remain aligned with us against Polynesia.
    - #6:
      - toolName: set-relationship
      - args:
        - TargetID: 2
        - Private: 50
        - Public: 50
        - Rationale: Venice is a friendly trade partner and also at war with Polynesia. Strengthening our relationship encourages their cooperation and trade, supporting our war effort.
    - #7:
      - toolName: set-flavors
      - args:

We are significantly reducing Nuke and UseNuke flavors to 10 to avoid the catastrophic and indiscriminate harm associated with nuclear weapons. We are slightly increasing the Culture flavor to 40 to help adopt our critical Autocracy tenets faster, while maintaining our high focus on Offense, Mobilization, Production, Gold, and Happiness to sustain our war effort.

  - messages:
    - #0:
      - role: assistant
      - content:
        - #0:
          - type: reasoning
          - text: **Assessing Our Situation**
            
            My focus is on our current standing; despite a leading score and Modern Era presence, our unhappy populace is imposing a significant combat penalty. This critical issue demands immediate attention.
            
            **Analyzing Current Conflicts**
            
            My current thinking centers on the ongoing war with Polynesia and its impact. The high war weariness on our side is concerning, especially with occupied cities exacerbating our unhappiness and combat penalties. I'm evaluating the implications of our chosen Autocracy policy in this context.
            
            **Evaluating Our Options**
            
            My current thoughts are on how to leverage our alliance with America to counter Polynesia. Their considerable military strength and shared Autocracy path present an opportunity. I'm weighing how to best exploit this advantage while managing our own war weariness and the immediate need to secure a Domination victory.
            
            **Prioritizing Next Research**
            
            I'm currently reassessing our technological trajectory, specifically the immediate benefits of researching Ballistics versus the long-term advantages of Corporations or Biology. The urgency of bolstering our modern military with Machine Guns and Anti-Aircraft Guns seems paramount given our ongoing conflict, but I'm weighing this against the potential for significant economic or strategic shifts offered by other advancements.
            
            **Reviewing Policy Options**
            
            I'm currently examining our available Autocracy tenets, particularly Martial Spirit, Third Alternative, and Lightning Warfare, to determine the most impactful choice. This decision is critical as I weigh the immediate combat boosts against long-term strategic gains, considering our ongoing war weariness.
            
            **Addressing Unhappiness**
            
            My current thinking is to prioritize happiness improvements. Specifically, I'm focusing on implementing the "Police State" tenet to boost local happiness from courthouses, which will be crucial given our many occupied cities. I'm also considering "New World Order" for broader unhappiness reduction.
            
            **Selecting New Policies**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on improving our unhappiness situation by reviewing the available Autocracy tenets. I'm evaluating whether "New World Order" or "Police State" would be a more effective immediate replacement for "Total War," considering the severe combat penalties we're experiencing due to low happiness.
            
            **Confirming Tenet Eligibility**
            
            I'm confirming my understanding that having two Level 1 Autocracy tenets, specifically Military-Industrial Complex and United Front, makes me eligible to adopt a Level 2 tenet. This means I can now pursue options like Police State, which appears highly beneficial given our occupied cities.
            
            **Adopting Police State**
            
            My current thinking confirms that all five occupied cities already possess courthouses. Adopting the "Police State" tenet will therefore grant an immediate and substantial +15 happiness, effectively resolving our current unhappiness crisis and lifting the severe combat penalties. This seems to be the most impactful immediate gain.
            
            **Selecting Optimal Policy**
            
            My current thinking confirms that "Police State" is the most advantageous policy to adopt next. Its direct happiness boost from courthouses, present in all my occupied cities, will immediately alleviate the critical unhappiness crisis and its associated combat penalties, overriding the tactical advantages of other options for now.
            
            **Securing Air Dominance**
            
            My current thoughts are now heavily focused on researching Corporations, as the Flight technology it unlocks is paramount for gaining air superiority. This will be crucial for both engaging the Polynesian islands and potentially breaking any stalemates in our ongoing war.
            
            **Leveraging Naval Power**
            
            My current thoughts are focused on the strategic value of our naval construction. With several coastal cities actively building Ironclads and Cruisers, we are well-positioned to engage Polynesia's significant navy, especially since their capital, Honolulu, is vulnerable to naval assault and our naval strength appears superior.
            
            **Focusing on Honolulu**
            
            My current focus is on the imminent capture of Honolulu. With our forces amassed and occupying key Polynesian cities, taking their capital is within reach and critical for our domination victory path. This strategic objective overrides other considerations.
            
            **Adopting Police State**
            
            I've confirmed that adopting "Police State" is the most effective immediate move. With courthouses in all occupied cities, this tenet will provide a substantial happiness boost, resolving our critical unhappiness crisis and the associated combat penalties.
            
            **Researching Technology**
            
            My current thinking is to prioritize researching Corporations to unlock Flight, which is essential for air superiority against Polynesia. This move is crucial for engaging their islands and breaking any ongoing war stalemates.
            
            **Strengthening Alliance**
            
            I'm focusing on our alliance with America. Their substantial military and shared Autocracy ideology are vital assets against Polynesia. I will ensure our relationship is maintained at its highest level, Public 100, Private 100, to solidify this crucial partnership and secure their aid.
            
            **Assessing Naval Strength**
            
            My current thoughts are on our robust naval construction. With Ironclads and Cruisers actively being built, we're well-positioned to challenge Polynesia's navy and target their capital, Honolulu, which is susceptible to naval assault.
            
            **Targeting Honolulu**
            
            My immediate focus is on capturing Honolulu. With our forces positioned and other key Polynesian cities occupied, taking their capital is a critical objective for achieving a domination victory.
            
            **Addressing Unhappiness**
            
            My current thinking confirms that adopting "Police State" is the most effective immediate move. With courthouses in all occupied cities, this tenet will provide a substantial happiness boost, resolving our critical unhappiness crisis and the associated combat penalties.
            
            **Analyzing Ideological Alignments**
            
            My current thoughts are on the complex web of ideological alliances and rivalries. With America as our sole Autocracy ally against the Order-aligned Korea and Maya, and Polynesia's lone Freedom stance, our diplomatic landscape is significantly shaped by these diverging paths. Polynesia's widespread conflicts are a critical observation.
            
            **Voting in Congress**
            
            My current thoughts are on the critical World Congress vote. Polynesia's "Sanction: Sweden" proposal is a major threat to our trade and happiness. Fortunately, America has proposed "Sanction: Polynesia," which we must support. I'm focused on ensuring our allies, particularly America, Maya, and Denmark, vote with us to defeat the sanctions against us and pass our counter-proposal, maximizing diplomatic favor.
            
            **Securing Diplomatic Favor**
            
            I'm currently strategizing our approach to the World Congress, focusing on defeating Polynesia's "Sanction: Sweden" and ensuring America's "Sanction: Polynesia" passes. I'm meticulously adjusting our relationships with allies like America, Denmark, Maya, and even neutral parties like Korea and Venice to secure the necessary votes.
            
            **Refining Persona Parameters**
            
            I'm currently reviewing our established persona values, particularly the low Loyalty setting. While it generally aligns with our aggressive conquest strategy and has facilitated Denmark's vassalization, I'm considering subtle adjustments to ensure it doesn't negatively impact our crucial alliance with America, especially as we navigate upcoming World Congress votes.
            
            **Analyzing Flavor Impacts**
            
            My thoughts are now focused on our extremely high game flavors. While generally beneficial, I'm examining how these exceptionally high ratings, particularly in military and production, might be impacting our current state. Despite the high happiness flavor, our actual happiness is at a critical 50%, necessitating immediate action.
            
            **Reinforcing Grand Strategy**
            
            My current thoughts are on explicitly reinforcing our "Conquest" grand strategy by calling `set-flavors`. I will ensure our flavors remain focused on Offense, Mobilization, Gold, and Production, while also addressing the critical need for Happiness, as observed in the recent flavor analysis.
            
            **Adjusting Flavors**
            
            I'm now recalibrating our strategic flavors. While continuing to prioritize Offense, Mobilization, Gold, and Production, I'm increasing Culture to 40 to accelerate policy adoption and ensuring Happiness remains at 100 to maximize building of relevant structures. I'm also noting the high Nuclear flavor, which warrants ethical consideration.
            
            **Adjusting Weapon Usage**
            
            I'm now setting the Nuke and UseNuke values to a more responsible 10. This reflects ethical considerations and a reduced emphasis on utilizing these powerful, potentially devastating weapons in our current strategic posture.
            
            **Adjusting Global Politics**
            
            I am now focusing on solidifying our alliances and countering threats in the World Congress. My current actions involve reinforcing our relationship with The Maya to ensure their continued support, as they are a crucial partner in our war with Polynesia. I will also be meticulously adjusting our flavors to prioritize conquest and happiness while ethically limiting nuclear weapon usage.
            
            **Researching for Advantage**
            
            I'm now prioritizing research into Flight and Radio. Flight is essential for gaining air superiority and executing coastal invasions against Polynesia, while Radio will bolster our cultural defense and ideological stability. I'm also reinforcing alliances with America, Denmark, and the Maya to secure support in the World Congress and maintain stability.
            
            **Reinforcing Grand Strategy**
            
            I am reinforcing our Conquest grand strategy by adjusting flavors. We'll maintain a strong focus on Offense, Mobilization, Production, Gold, and Happiness, while also increasing Culture to 40 to accelerate policy adoption and ethically reducing Nuke/UseNuke to 10. I'm also ensuring our alliance with The Maya remains strong to support our war effort.

        - #2:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: LCPcgNQ8fgr3OyeJ
          - toolName: set-research
          - input:
            - Technology: Corporations
            - Rationale: Setting Corporations as our next technology. This unlocks the pathway to Flight and Radio. Flight is vital for modern warfare, allowing us to establish air superiority and execute effective coastal invasions against Polynesia's island cities. Radio will also boost our cultural defense and ideological stability.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #3:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: YUKY6Zt0psg7beCt
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Public: 100
            - TargetID: 3
            - Private: 100
            - Rationale: America is our closest ally, sharing our Autocracy ideology, maintaining a Defensive Pact, and trading with us. We want to maximize our relationship to ensure they remain a steadfast partner in our global campaigns and support us in the World Congress against Polynesia's proposed sanctions.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #4:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: q1bvyep5fLw6HGEI
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Rationale: Denmark is our capitulated vassal. Maintaining maximum positive relationship is key to keeping them loyal, secure, and supportive of our military endeavors without risk of rebellion.
            - Private: 100
            - TargetID: 6
            - Public: 100
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #5:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: yQkxzLuGtTHgxDNY
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Rationale: The Maya are our friends, and we share a Defensive Pact and a Declaration of Friendship. Even though they adopted the Order ideology, they are actively at war with Polynesia and are a key trade partner. Keeping our relationship very friendly ensures they remain a strong ally against Kamehameha.
            - TargetID: 5
            - Private: 80
            - Public: 80
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #6:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: sd5mQ1DKk59CXEwb
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - Public: 50
            - Private: 50
            - TargetID: 7
            - Rationale: Korea is a friendly trade partner with whom we have open borders. Keeping our relationship warm helps maintain our economic agreements and ensures they remain aligned with us against Polynesia.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #7:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: LhI4linMsPlnciYA
          - toolName: set-relationship
          - input:
            - TargetID: 2
            - Private: 50
            - Public: 50
            - Rationale: Venice is a friendly trade partner and also at war with Polynesia. Strengthening our relationship encourages their cooperation and trade, supporting our war effort.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
        - #8:
          - type: tool-call
          - toolCallId: 9XBtkAHnyMPqhN5J
          - toolName: set-flavors
          - input:
            - Flavors:
              - MilitaryTraining: 100
              - Gold: 100
              - Science: 70
              - Recon: 95
              - Mobilization: 100
              - TileImprovement: 85
              - Culture: 40
              - Naval: 100
              - NavalGrowth: 100
              - GreatPeople: 100
              - Production: 100
              - AirCarrier: 100
              - Air: 100
              - Antiair: 50
              - Infrastructure: 80
              - Nuke: 10
              - NavalTileImprovement: 100
              - NavalRecon: 80
              - Mobile: 80
              - Defense: 80
              - Ranged: 90
              - Offense: 90
              - Expansion: 0
              - UseNuke: 10
              - CityDefense: 90
              - Growth: 50
              - WaterConnection: 85
              - Wonder: 80
              - Happiness: 100
            - GrandStrategy: Conquest
            - Rationale: We are reinforcing our Grand Strategy of Conquest as we press toward Honolulu to capture Polynesia's capital. We are significantly reducing Nuke and UseNuke flavors to 10 to avoid the catastrophic and indiscriminate harm associated with nuclear weapons. We are slightly increasing the Culture flavor to 40 to help adopt our critical Autocracy tenets faster, while maintaining our high focus on Offense, Mobilization, Production, Gold, and Happiness to sustain our war effort.
          - providerExecuted: undefined
          - providerOptions: undefined
